L^{2}(G)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

The
cutoff operator
with respect to
\chi
is
[\chi]:L^{2}(G)\to L^{2}(G)
defined by
([\chi]\psi)(g)=\chi(g)\psi(g)
for every
\psi\in L^{2}(G)\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss{,}}\hskip 1.66669pt\mspace{3muplus0muminus3mu}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss{,}}\hskip 1.66669pt\mspace{3muplus0muminus3mu}}{\textup{,}\mspace{2muplus0muminus2mu}}{\textup{,}\mspace{1muplus0muminus1mu}}g\in G\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Let
A
and
B
be operators on
L^{2}(G)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

The
commutator operator
with respect to
A
and
B
is
[A,B]:L^{2}(G)\to L^{2}(G)
defined by
([A,B]\psi)(g)=((AB-BA)\psi)(g)
for every
\psi\in L^{2}(G)\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss{,}}\hskip 1.66669pt\mspace{3muplus0muminus3mu}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss{,}}\hskip 1.66669pt\mspace{3muplus0muminus3mu}}{\textup{,}\mspace{2muplus0muminus2mu}}{\textup{,}\mspace{1muplus0muminus1mu}}g\in G\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Lemma 4.2
.

Assume
\langle G,\lambda\rangle
is unimodular.
Let
\chi
be a compactly-supported continuous nonzero vector.
Define, for
j,h\in G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\SwapAboveDisplaySkip\chi_{j}:

\displaystyle G\to\mathbb{C}:g\mapsto\chi(j^{\smash{-1}}g)\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\cramped{\chi^{h}}:

\displaystyle G\to\mathbb{C}:g\mapsto\chi(gh)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Fix
x\in Z
and
\psi\in L^{2}(G)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Then

\displaystyle\int\lVert[A_{x},[\chi_{j}]]\psi\rVert^{2}dj\leq

\displaystyle(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)^{2}\lVert\psi\rVert^{2}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\int\lVert[A_{x}\!{}^{*},[\chi_{j}]]\psi\rVert^{2}dj\leq

\displaystyle(\int\lVert k(h^{\smash{-1}},(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)^{2}\lVert\psi\rVert^{2}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\int\lVert[\chi_{j}]\psi\rVert^{2}dj=

\displaystyle\lVert\chi\rVert^{2}\lVert\psi\rVert^{2}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Proof.
Observe

\displaystyle\int\lVert[A_{x},[\chi_{j}]]\psi\rVert^{2}dj

\displaystyle=\iint\lvert\int k(h,g^{\smash{-1}}x)(\chi_{j}(gh)-\chi_{j}(g))\psi(gh)dh\rvert^{2}dgdj

\displaystyle\leq\iint(\int\lvert k(h,g^{\smash{-1}}x)\rvert\lvert\chi_{j}(gh)-\chi_{j}(g)\rvert\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert dh)^{2}dgdj

\mathllap{\smash{(\begin{subarray}{l}\textrm{Hölder's}\\
\textrm{inequality}\end{subarray})}}

\displaystyle\leq\iint\!\begin{array}[t]{l}(\int\lvert k(h,g^{\smash{-1}}x)\rvert\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(gh)\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)\\
(\int\lvert k(h,g^{\smash{-1}}x)\rvert\lvert\chi_{j}(gh)-\chi_{j}(g)\rvert^{2}\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert^{2}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert^{-1}dh)dgdj\end{array}

\displaystyle\leq\iint\!\begin{array}[t]{l}(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)\\
(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\lvert\chi_{j}(gh)-\chi_{j}(g)\rvert^{2}\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert^{2}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert^{-1}dh)djdg\end{array}

\displaystyle=(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)^{2}\lVert\psi\rVert^{2}

and

\displaystyle\int\lVert[A_{x}\!{}^{*},[\chi_{j}]]\psi\rVert^{2}dj

\displaystyle=\iint\lvert\int\overline{k(h^{\smash{-1}},h^{\smash{-1}}g^{\smash{-1}}x)}(\chi_{j}(gh)-\chi_{j}(g))\psi(gh)dh\rvert^{2}dgdj

\displaystyle\leq\iint(\int\lvert\overline{k(h^{\smash{-1}},h^{\smash{-1}}g^{\smash{-1}}x)}\rvert\lvert\chi_{j}(gh)-\chi_{j}(g)\rvert\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert dh)^{2}dgdj

\displaystyle\leq\iint\!\begin{array}[t]{l}(\int\lvert k(h^{\smash{-1}},(gh)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rvert\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(gh)\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)\\
(\int\lvert k(h^{\smash{-1}},(gh)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rvert\lvert\chi_{j}(gh)-\chi_{j}(g)\rvert^{2}\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert^{2}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert^{-1}dh)dgdj\end{array}

\displaystyle\leq\iint\!\begin{array}[t]{l}(\int\lVert k(h^{\smash{-1}},(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)\\
(\int\lVert k(h^{\smash{-1}},(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\lvert\chi_{j}(gh)-\chi_{j}(g)\rvert^{2}\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert^{2}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert^{-1}dh)djdg\end{array}

\displaystyle=(\int\lVert k(h^{\smash{-1}},(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)^{2}\lVert\psi\rVert^{2}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Also,

\textstyle\int\lVert[\chi_{j}]\psi\rVert^{2}dj=\iint\lvert\chi_{j}(g)\rvert^{2}\lvert\psi(g)\rvert^{2}dgdj=\iint\lvert\chi_{j}(g)\rvert^{2}\lvert\psi(g)\rvert^{2}djdg=\lVert\chi\rVert^{2}\lVert\psi\rVert^{2}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}\qed

Lemma 4.3
.

Assume
\langle G,\lambda\rangle
is unimodular and
A_{x}
is normal for every
x\in Z\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Let
\chi
be a compactly-supported continuous nonzero vector.
Fix
X,Y\in\mathscr{S}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Then for each
\rho>0\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle\Delta_{\mathsf{H}}\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{x}}\in X,\smash[t]{\check{y}}\in Y,\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\\
\mathrlap{d(\smash[t]{\check{x}},\smash[t]{\check{y}})<\delta_{\mathsf{H}}+\rho}\end{subarray}}\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{y}})-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{x}}))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\chi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

where
\Delta_{\mathsf{H}}\coloneqq\dist_{\mathsf{H}}(\spec(A_{X}),\spec(A_{Y}))
and
\delta_{\mathsf{H}}\coloneqq d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)
and
\cramped{\chi^{h}}:G\to\mathbb{C}:g\mapsto\chi(gh)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Proof.
Fix
\rho>0\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Let
E\in\spec(A_{X})\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Let
\varepsilon>0\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe there exist
x\in X
and
E^{\prime}\in\spec(A_{x})
such that
\lvert E-E^{\prime}\rvert<\varepsilon\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Without loss of generality, assume
E^{\prime}\notin\spec(A_{Y})\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe
A_{x}
is normal and, by Weyl’s criterion, there exists a nonzero vector
\varphi
such that
\lVert(A_{x}-E^{\prime})\varphi\rVert<\varepsilon\lVert\varphi\rVert\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Without loss of generality, assume
\varphi
is both continuous and compactly-supported.
Define, for
j\in G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle\chi_{j}:G\to\mathbb{C}:g\mapsto\chi(j^{\smash{-1}}g)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Define
r\coloneqq\smash{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe

\displaystyle\int\lVert(A_{x}-E^{\prime})[\chi_{j}]\varphi\rVert^{2}dj

\displaystyle=\int\lVert[(A_{x}-E^{\prime}),[\chi_{j}]]\varphi+[\chi_{j}](A_{x}-E^{\prime})\varphi\rVert^{2}dj

\mathllap{\smash{(\begin{subarray}{l}\textrm{Peter--Paul's}\\
\textrm{inequality}\end{subarray})}}

\displaystyle\leq\int(1+\smash{\frac{1}{r}})\lVert[(A_{x}-E^{\prime}),[\chi_{j}]]\varphi\rVert^{2}+(1+r)\lVert[\chi_{j}](A_{x}-E^{\prime})\varphi\rVert^{2}dj

\mathllap{\smash{(\begin{subarray}{l}\textrm{Lem~\ref{lem:po(2)}}\end{subarray})}}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\smash{\frac{1}{r}})(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)^{2}\lVert\varphi\rVert^{2}+(1+r)\lVert\chi\rVert^{2}\lVert(A_{x}-E^{\prime})\varphi\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle<(1+\smash{\frac{1}{r}})(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)^{2}\lVert\varphi\rVert^{2}+(1+r)\varepsilon^{2}\lVert\chi\rVert^{2}\lVert\varphi\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\int(1+\smash{\frac{1}{r}})(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert dh)^{2}\lVert\chi\rVert^{-2}\lVert[\chi_{j}]\varphi\rVert^{2}+(1+r)\varepsilon^{2}\lVert[\chi_{j}]\varphi\rVert^{2}dj

\displaystyle=\int\Big((1+\smash{\frac{1}{r}})(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh)^{2}+(1+r)\varepsilon^{2}\Big)\lVert[\chi_{j}]\varphi\rVert^{2}dj

\displaystyle=\int(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh+\varepsilon)^{2}\lVert[\chi_{j}]\varphi\rVert^{2}dj\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe there exists
j\in G
such that

\textstyle\lVert(A_{x}-E^{\prime})[\chi_{j}]\varphi\rVert<(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}dh+\varepsilon)\lVert[\chi_{j}]\varphi\rVert\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Define
\varphi_{j}\coloneqq[\chi_{j}]\varphi\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe

\textstyle\supp(\varphi_{j})\subseteq\supp(\chi_{j})\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe there exists
y\in Y
such that
d(j^{\smash{-1}}x,j^{\smash{-1}}y)<d(j^{\smash{-1}}x,Y)+\rho\leq\delta_{\mathsf{H}}+\rho
since
Y
is left-invariant.
Also,
A_{y}
is normal and

\textstyle\lVert(A_{y}-E^{\prime})\varphi_{j}\rVert=\lVert(A_{y}-E^{\prime})^{*}\varphi_{j}\rVert<(\lVert(A_{y}-A_{x})^{*}\smash{\frac{\varphi_{j}}{\lVert\varphi_{j}\rVert}}\rVert+\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh+\varepsilon)\lVert\varphi_{j}\rVert

and

\displaystyle\dist(E,\spec(A_{Y}))

\displaystyle<\dist(E^{\prime},\spec(A_{y}))+\varepsilon

\displaystyle=\lVert(A_{y}-E^{\prime})^{-1}\rVert^{-1}+\varepsilon

\displaystyle\leq\lVert(A_{y}-E^{\prime})\smash{\frac{\varphi_{j}}{\lVert\varphi_{j}\rVert}}\rVert+\varepsilon

\displaystyle<\lVert(A_{y}-A_{x})^{*}\smash{\frac{\varphi_{j}}{\lVert\varphi_{j}\rVert}}\rVert+\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh+2\varepsilon

\displaystyle\leq\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\varphi_{j})}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh+2\varepsilon

\displaystyle\leq\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\chi_{j})}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh+2\varepsilon

\displaystyle=\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}j^{\smash{-1}}y)-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}j^{\smash{-1}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\chi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh+2\varepsilon

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{x}}\in X,\smash[t]{\check{y}}\in Y,\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\\
\mathrlap{d(\smash[t]{\check{x}},\smash[t]{\check{y}})<\delta_{\mathsf{H}}+\rho}\end{subarray}}\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{y}})-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{x}}))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\chi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh+2\varepsilon\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

As a result,
\sup_{E\in\spec(A_{X})}\dist(E,\spec(A_{Y}))
and
\Delta_{\mathsf{H}}
are bounded from above by

\textstyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{x}}\in X,\smash[t]{\check{y}}\in Y,\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\\
\mathrlap{d(\smash[t]{\check{x}},\smash[t]{\check{y}})<\delta_{\mathsf{H}}+\rho}\end{subarray}}\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{y}})-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{x}}))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\chi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}dh

as desired.
∎

Theorem 4.4
.

Assume
\langle G,\lambda\rangle
is unimodular,
A_{x}
is normal for every
x\in Z\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
and the following.

(i)

\alpha
satisfies a uniform continuity condition:
for each
\varepsilon>0
and for each nonempty compact subset
K
of
G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
there exists
\delta>0
such that

d(y,x)<\delta\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\Rightarrow\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\sup_{g\in K}d(g^{\smash{-1}}y,g^{\smash{-1}}x)<\varepsilon\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(ii)

k
satisfies a uniform continuity condition:
for each
\varepsilon>0
and for each nonempty compact subset
F
of
G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
there exists
\delta>0
such that

d(y,x)<\delta\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\Rightarrow\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}{\int\lvert k(h,y)-k(h,x)\rvert\mathbbm{1}_{F}(h)dh}<\varepsilon\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(iii)

k
satisfies a uniform decay condition:
for each
\varepsilon>0\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
there exists a nonempty compact subset
F
of
G
such that

\sup_{x\in Z}\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{G\setminus F}(h)\rVert_{\infty}dh<\varepsilon\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(iv)

\langle G,\lambda\rangle
is amenable.

Then for each
\varepsilon>0\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
there exists
\delta>0
such that for all
X,Y\in\mathscr{S}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
if
d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)<\delta\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
then
\dist_{\mathsf{H}}(\spec(A_{X}),\spec(A_{Y}))<\varepsilon\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
i.e.,

\textstyle\lim_{\delta\to 0^{+}}\sup_{X,Y\in\mathscr{S},d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)<\delta}\dist_{\mathsf{H}}(\spec(A_{X}),\spec(A_{Y}))=0\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Proof.
Fix
\varepsilon>0\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe there exists a nonempty compact subset
F
of
G
such that

\textstyle\sup_{x\in Z}\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{G\setminus F}(h)\rVert_{\infty}dh<\smash{\frac{1}{6}}\varepsilon

since
k
satisfies a uniform decay condition.
Define
\lVert k\rVert\coloneqq\sup_{x\in Z}\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}dh\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe there exists a compactly-supported continuous nonzero vector
\chi\in L^{2}_{+}(G)
such that

\textstyle\sup_{h\in F}\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}<\smash{\frac{1}{6(\lVert k\rVert+1)}}\varepsilon\begin{comment}

since
\langle G,\lambda\rangle
is amenable; see
section
3.1.2
.
Here
\cramped{\chi^{h}}:G\to[0,+\infty):g\mapsto\chi(gh)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Define
K\coloneqq\supp(\chi)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe there exists
\delta>0
such that

\textstyle d(y,x)<2\delta\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\Rightarrow\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{K}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\mathbbm{1}_{F}(h)dh<\smash{\frac{1}{6}}\varepsilon

since the group action and
k
satisfy a uniform continuity condition.
Fix
X,Y\in\mathscr{S}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Assume
d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)<\delta\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe

\displaystyle\dist_{\mathsf{H}}(\spec(A_{X}),\spec(A_{Y}))

\mathllap{\smash{{\begin{subarray}{l}(\begin{subarray}{l}\textrm{Lem~\ref{lem:po(3)}}\end{subarray})\end{subarray}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{x}}\in X,\smash[t]{\check{y}}\in Y,\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\\
\mathrlap{d(\smash[t]{\check{x}},\smash[t]{\check{y}})<d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)+\delta}\end{subarray}}\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{y}})-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{x}}))\mathbbm{1}_{K}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}dh

\displaystyle\leq\begin{array}[t]{l}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{x}}\in X,\smash[t]{\check{y}}\in Y,\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\\
\mathrlap{d(\smash[t]{\check{x}},\smash[t]{\check{y}})<2\delta}\end{subarray}}\Big(\\
\int(\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{y}})-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{x}}))\mathbbm{1}_{K}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert})\mathbbm{1}_{F}(h)dh\\
+\int(\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{y}})-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{x}}))\mathbbm{1}_{K}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert})\mathbbm{1}_{G\setminus F}(h)dh\Big)\end{array}

\displaystyle<\smash{\frac{2}{6}}\varepsilon+\smash{\frac{4}{6}}\varepsilon\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}\qed

Remark 4.4.1
.

Theorem
4.4
will be improved provided the group action and
k
satisfy a Lipschitz continuity condition,
k
satisfies a linear decay condition, and the group has strict polynomial growth.

Lemma 4.5
.

Assume
\langle G,\lambda\rangle
is unimodular.
Let
r\geq 1\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Define

\textstyle\chi_{r}=\chi:G\to[0,+\infty):g\mapsto(\smash{\frac{r-\lvert g\rvert}{r}})\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)}(g)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Assume
\langle(G,\ell),\lambda\rangle
is strictly-polynomial-growing:
there exist
b\geq 1
and
c_{1}\geq c_{0}>0
such that for each
r\geq 1\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

c_{0}r^{b}\leq\lambda(B(r))\leq c_{1}r^{b}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Fix
h\in G\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Then

\textstyle\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert\leq\min\{\smash{\frac{\lvert h\rvert}{r}}(\smash{\frac{(2+b)^{2+b}c_{1}}{2b^{b}c_{0}}})^{\smash{\frac{1}{2}}},2^{\smash{\frac{1}{2}}}\}\lVert\chi\rVert\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

where
\cramped{\chi^{h}}:G\to[0,+\infty):g\mapsto\chi(gh)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Proof.
Define
r^{\prime}\coloneqq(\smash{\frac{2}{2+b}})r\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe

\displaystyle\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}\setminus B(r)}(\smash{\frac{r-\lvert gh\rvert}{r}})^{2}dg+\int_{B(r)\setminus B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\smash{\frac{r-\lvert g\rvert}{r}})^{2}dg+\int_{B(r)\cap B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\smash{\frac{\lvert g\rvert-\lvert gh\rvert}{r}})^{2}dg

\displaystyle\leq\int_{B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}\setminus B(r)}(\smash{\frac{\lvert g\rvert-\lvert gh\rvert}{r}})^{2}dg+\int_{B(r)\setminus B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\smash{\frac{\lvert gh\rvert-\lvert g\rvert}{r}})^{2}dg+\int_{B(r)\cap B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\smash{\frac{\lvert g\rvert-\lvert gh\rvert}{r}})^{2}dg

\displaystyle\leq\int_{B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}\setminus B(r)}r^{-2}\lvert h\rvert^{2}dg+\int_{B(r)\setminus B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}}r^{-2}\lvert h\rvert^{2}dg+\int_{B(r)\cap B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}}r^{-2}\lvert h\rvert^{2}dg

\displaystyle\leq r^{-2}\lvert h\rvert^{2}2(c_{1}r^{b})

and

\displaystyle\lVert\chi\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{B(r)\setminus B(r-r^{\prime})}(\smash{\frac{r-\lvert g\rvert}{r}})^{2}dg+\int_{B(r-r^{\prime})}(\smash{\frac{r-\lvert g\rvert}{r}})^{2}dg

\displaystyle\geq\int_{B(r-r^{\prime})}(\smash{\frac{r-\lvert g\rvert}{r}})^{2}dg

\displaystyle\geq\int_{B(r-r^{\prime})}r^{-2}(r^{\prime})^{2}dg

\displaystyle\geq r^{-2}(r^{\prime})^{2}(c_{0}(r-r^{\prime})^{b})\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Also,

\textstyle\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert^{2}\leq\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}\rVert^{2}+\lVert\chi\rVert^{2}=2\lVert\chi\rVert^{2}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}\qed

Remark 4.5.1
.

(a)

For each nonempty compact subset
F
of
G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\sup_{h\in F}{\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}}\to 0
as
r\to+\infty\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(b)

If
G
is discrete, then

\displaystyle\SwapAboveDisplaySkip\lVert\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)}\rVert^{2}=

\displaystyle\#B(r)\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\lVert\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}\!}-\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)}\rVert^{2}=

\displaystyle\#(B(r)h^{\smash{-1}}\!\bigtriangleup B(r))\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(c)

Define
\smash[t]{\acute{r}}\coloneqq\max\{n\in\mathbb{Z}:0\leq n<r\}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

If
G=\mathbb{Z}
, then

\displaystyle\lVert\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)}\rVert^{2}=

\displaystyle 2\smash[t]{\acute{r}}+1\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\lVert\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)-m\!}-\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)}\rVert^{2}=

\displaystyle\min\{2\lvert m\rvert,2(2\smash[t]{\acute{r}}+1)\}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\smash{\frac{\lVert\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)-m\!}-\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)}\rVert}{\lVert\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)}\rVert}}=

\displaystyle O(r^{-\smash{\frac{1}{2}}})\big|_{m\in\mathbb{Z},r\to+\infty}

but

\displaystyle\lVert\chi\rVert^{2}=

\displaystyle\smash{\frac{2}{3}}(\smash{\frac{(2\smash[t]{\acute{r}}+1)(\smash[t]{\acute{r}}+1)}{2r^{2}}}+\smash{\frac{3(r-\smash[t]{\acute{r}}-1)}{r}})\smash[t]{\acute{r}}+1\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\lVert\cramped{\chi^{m}}-\chi\rVert^{2}=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\begin{subarray}{l}\cramped{0}\end{subarray}&\begin{subarray}{l}\textup{if}~m=0\end{subarray}\\
\begin{subarray}{l}\cramped{\smash{\frac{m^{2}}{r}}\big(2-\smash{\frac{m}{r}}+\smash{\frac{2(r-\smash[t]{\acute{r}})^{2}-2(r-\smash[t]{\acute{r}})+1}{mr}}\big)}\end{subarray}&\begin{subarray}{l}\textup{if}~1\leq m\leq\smash[t]{\acute{r}}\end{subarray}\\
\begin{subarray}{l}\cramped{\smash{\frac{m}{3}}\big((\smash{\frac{m}{r}})^{2}-6(\smash{\frac{m}{r}})+12(\smash{\frac{\smash[t]{\acute{r}}}{r}})\big)-\smash{\frac{\smash[t]{\acute{r}}}{3}}\big(4(\smash{\frac{\smash[t]{\acute{r}}}{r}})\big)}\\
\cramped{\hphantom{\smash{\frac{m}{3}}}+\smash{\frac{m}{3}}\big(6(1-(\smash{\frac{\smash[t]{\acute{r}}}{r}})^{2})+6(1-\smash{\frac{\smash[t]{\acute{r}}}{r}})(\smash{\frac{1}{r}})-(\smash{\frac{1}{r}})^{2}\big)-\smash{\frac{\smash[t]{\acute{r}}}{3}}\big(4(2\smash[t]{\acute{r}}+1)(1-\smash{\frac{\smash[t]{\acute{r}}}{r}})(\smash{\frac{1}{r}})-(\smash{\frac{1}{r}})^{2}\big)}\end{subarray}&\begin{subarray}{l}\textup{if}~\smash[t]{\acute{r}}+1\leq m\leq 2\smash[t]{\acute{r}}\end{subarray}\\
\begin{subarray}{l}\cramped{2\lVert\chi\rVert^{2}}\end{subarray}&\begin{subarray}{l}\textup{if}~2\smash[t]{\acute{r}}+1\leq m\end{subarray}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\smash{\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{m}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}}=

\displaystyle O(r^{-1})\big|_{m\in\mathbb{Z},r\to+\infty}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(d)

Lemma
4.5
establishes that strict polynomial growth implies amenability; see Theorem
3.4
and part (
a
) above.
In the case where
G
is discrete, one could consider indicator functions on a Følner sequence instead of tent functions—which requires a metric to define; see part (
b
) above.
An advantage of using tent functions instead of indicator functions on balls
B(r)
is that the limiting behavior is of order
r^{-1}
which is sharp; see Lemma
4.5
and part (
c
) above.

(e)

In the case where
G=\mathbb{Z}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
a 1990 result by M. Choi; G.A. Elliott; N. Yui
[
27
]
establishes spectral
\smash{\frac{1}{3}}
-Hölder continuity for one-dimensional discrete quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators with Lipschitz continuous potentials, e.g., the Almost Mathieu Operator, by utilizing indicator functions.
Within the same year, J. Avron; P.H.M.v. Mouche; B. Simon
[
6
]
improved the result and established spectral
\smash{\frac{1}{2}}
-Hölder continuity by utilizing tent functions.

Theorem 4.6
.

Assume
\langle G,\lambda\rangle
is unimodular,
A_{x}
is normal for every
x\in Z\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
and the following.

(i)

\alpha
satisfies a Lipschitz continuity condition:
there exists
c_{\alpha}>0
such that for each
g\in G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle d(g^{\smash{-1}}x,g^{\smash{-1}}y)\leq(c_{\alpha}\lvert g\rvert+1)d(x,y)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(ii)

k
satisfies a Lipschitz continuity condition:
there exists
c_{k}\in L^{1}_{+}(G)
such that for
\lambda
-a.e.
h\in G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle\lvert k(h,x)-k(h,y)\rvert\leq c_{k}(h)d(x,y)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(iii)

k
satisfies a linear decay condition:
there exists
c_{s}>0
such that for each
r\geq 0\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle\sup_{x\in Z}\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\min\{\lvert h\rvert,r\}\rVert_{\infty}dh\leq c_{s}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(iv)

\langle(G,\ell),\lambda\rangle
is strictly-polynomial-growing:
there exist
b\geq 1
and
c_{1}\geq c_{0}>0
such that for each
r\geq 1\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle c_{0}r^{b}\leq\lambda(B(r))\leq c_{1}r^{b}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Fix
X,Y\in\mathscr{S}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Then for each
r\geq 1\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle\dist_{\mathsf{H}}(\spec(A_{X}),\spec(A_{Y}))\leq\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{1}(c_{\alpha}r+1)d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)+\frac{c_{s}}{r}(\frac{(2+b)^{2+b}c_{1}}{2b^{b}c_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}

and

\textstyle d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)\leq{\min\{\delta,1\}}\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\Rightarrow\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\dist_{\mathsf{H}}(\spec(A_{X}),\spec(A_{Y}))\leq Cd_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

where
\delta\coloneqq\frac{c_{s}}{\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{1}c_{\alpha}}(\frac{(2+b)^{2+b}c_{1}}{2b^{b}c_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
C\coloneqq 2(\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{1}c_{\alpha}c_{s}(\frac{(2+b)^{2+b}c_{1}}{2b^{b}c_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{1}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Proof.
Fix
r\geq 1\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Define
\chi_{r}=\chi:G\to[0,+\infty):g\mapsto(\frac{r-\lvert g\rvert}{r})\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)}(g)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Let
\rho>0\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe

\displaystyle\dist_{\mathsf{H}}(\spec(A_{X}),\spec(A_{Y}))

\mathllap{\smash{{\begin{subarray}{l}(\begin{subarray}{l}\textrm{Lem~\ref{lem:po(3)}}\end{subarray})\end{subarray}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{x}}\in X,\smash[t]{\check{y}}\in Y,\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\\
\mathrlap{d(\smash[t]{\check{x}},\smash[t]{\check{y}})<d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)+\rho}\end{subarray}}\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{y}})-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{x}}))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\chi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}dh

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{x}}\in X,\smash[t]{\check{y}}\in Y,\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\\
\mathrlap{d(\smash[t]{\check{x}},\smash[t]{\check{y}})<d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)+\rho}\end{subarray}}\int\lVert c_{k}(h)\mspace{1muminus1mu}d((\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{y}},(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{x}})\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\chi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}dh

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{x}}\in X,\smash[t]{\check{y}}\in Y,\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\\
\mathrlap{d(\smash[t]{\check{x}},\smash[t]{\check{y}})<d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)+\rho}\end{subarray}}\int c_{k}(h)(c_{\alpha}r+1)d(\smash[t]{\check{y}},\smash[t]{\check{x}})+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}dh

\mathllap{\smash{{\begin{subarray}{l}(\begin{subarray}{l}\textrm{Lem~\ref{lem:po(5)}}\end{subarray})\end{subarray}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{1}(c_{\alpha}r+1)(d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)+\rho)+\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\end{subarray}}\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\min\{\frac{\lvert h\rvert}{r}(\frac{(2+b)^{2+b}c_{1}}{2b^{b}c_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}},2^{\frac{1}{2}}\}dh

\displaystyle\leq\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{1}(c_{\alpha}r+1)(d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)+\rho)+\frac{c_{s}}{r}(\frac{(2+b)^{2+b}c_{1}}{2b^{b}c_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Here
\cramped{\chi^{h}}:G\to[0,+\infty):g\mapsto\chi(gh)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Also, if

\textstyle d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)\leq\min\{\delta,1\}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

then
1\leq(d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)^{-1}\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)\leq d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)^{\frac{1}{2}}
and

\displaystyle\dist_{\mathsf{H}}(\spec(A_{X}),\spec(A_{Y}))

\displaystyle\leq\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{1}(c_{\alpha}r+1)d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)+\frac{c_{s}}{r}(\frac{(2+b)^{2+b}c_{1}}{2b^{b}c_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big|_{r=(d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)^{-1}\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}\qed

Theorem 4.7
.

Assume
\langle G,\lambda\rangle
is unimodular,
A_{x}
is normal for every
x\in Z\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
and the following.

(i)

\alpha
satisfies a Lipschitz continuity condition:
there exists
c_{\alpha}>0
such that for each
g\in G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle d(g^{\smash{-1}}x,g^{\smash{-1}}y)\leq(c_{\alpha}\lvert g\rvert+1)d(x,y)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(ii)

k
satisfies a locally-constant condition:
there exists
c_{k}\in L^{\infty}_{+}(G)
such that for
\lambda
-a.e.
h\in G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle d(x,y)<\frac{1}{c_{k}(h)}\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\Rightarrow\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}k(h,x)=k(h,y)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(iii)

k
satisfies a linear decay condition:
there exists
c_{s}>0
such that for each
r\geq 0\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle\sup_{x\in Z}\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\min\{\lvert h\rvert,r\}\rVert_{\infty}dh\leq c_{s}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(iv)

\langle(G,\ell),\lambda\rangle
is strictly-polynomial-growing:
there exist
b\geq 1
and
c_{1}\geq c_{0}>0
such that for each
r\geq 1\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\textstyle c_{0}r^{b}\leq\lambda(B(r))\leq c_{1}r^{b}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Fix
X,Y\in\mathscr{S}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Then

\textstyle d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)\leq\delta\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\Rightarrow\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\dist_{\mathsf{H}}(\spec(A_{X}),\spec(A_{Y}))\leq Cd_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

where
\delta\coloneqq\frac{1}{(\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{\infty}+1)(c_{\alpha}+1)}
and
C\coloneqq(\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{\infty}+1)(c_{\alpha}+1)c_{s}(\frac{(2+b)^{2+b}c_{1}}{2b^{b}c_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Proof.
Assume
d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)\leq\delta\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{\infty}+1)\delta c_{\alpha}}-\frac{1}{c_{\alpha}}=

\displaystyle 1\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\frac{c_{\alpha}}{1-(\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{\infty}+1)d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)}\leq

\displaystyle c_{\alpha}+1\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Define
r\coloneqq\frac{1}{(\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{\infty}+1)d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)c_{\alpha}}-\frac{1}{c_{\alpha}}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe

\displaystyle r\geq

\displaystyle 1\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\smash{\frac{1}{r}}\leq

\displaystyle(\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{\infty}+1)(c_{\alpha}+1)d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Define, for
h\in G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\SwapAboveDisplaySkip\chi_{r}=\chi:

\displaystyle G\to[0,+\infty):g\mapsto(\frac{r-\lvert g\rvert}{r})\mathbbm{1}_{B(r)}(g)\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\cramped{\chi^{h}}:

\displaystyle G\to[0,+\infty):g\mapsto\chi(gh)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Define
\rho\coloneqq\frac{d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)}{\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{\infty}}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe

\textstyle d(x,y)<d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)+\rho\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\Rightarrow\mspace{4muplus0muminus4mu}\lVert d((\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\chi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\leq(c_{\alpha}r+1)d(x,y)<\frac{1}{\lVert c_{k}\rVert_{\infty}}

and

\displaystyle\dist_{\mathsf{H}}(\spec(A_{X}),\spec(A_{Y}))

\mathllap{\smash{{\begin{subarray}{l}(\begin{subarray}{l}\textrm{Lem~\ref{lem:po(3)}}\end{subarray})\end{subarray}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{x}}\in X,\smash[t]{\check{y}}\in Y,\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\\
\mathrlap{d(\smash[t]{\check{x}},\smash[t]{\check{y}})<d_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)+\rho}\end{subarray}}\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{y}})-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{x}}))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\chi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}dh

\displaystyle\leq 0+\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\end{subarray}}\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\lVert\cramped{\chi^{h}}-\chi\rVert}{\lVert\chi\rVert}dh

\mathllap{\smash{{\begin{subarray}{l}(\begin{subarray}{l}\textrm{Lem~\ref{lem:po(5)}}\end{subarray})\end{subarray}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{l}\smash[t]{\check{z}}\in Z\end{subarray}}\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}\smash[t]{\check{z}})\rVert_{\infty}\min\{\frac{\lvert h\rvert}{r}(\frac{(2+b)^{2+b}c_{1}}{2b^{b}c_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}},2^{\frac{1}{2}}\}dh

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{s}}{r}(\frac{(2+b)^{2+b}c_{1}}{2b^{b}c_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{\mathsf{H}}(X,Y)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}\qed

5.
Proof of Lemma
3.2

Let
\langle G,\lambda\rangle
be a second-countable locally-compact Hausdorff group endowed with a left Haar measure.
Let
\langle Z,(G,\alpha)\rangle
be a topological space endowed with a left continuous action.
Let
k:G\times Z\to\mathbb{C}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
where
k
is measurable and

\sup_{x\in Z}\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\rVert_{\infty}dh<+\infty\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Define, for
x\in Z\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

{A}_{x}:L^{2}(G)\to L^{2}(G)
by

\textstyle(A_{x}\psi)(g)=\int k(h,g^{\smash{-1}}x)\psi(gh)dh=\int k(g^{\smash{-1}}h,g^{\smash{-1}}x)\psi(h)dh

for every
\psi\in L^{2}(G)\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss{,}}\hskip 1.66669pt\mspace{3muplus0muminus3mu}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss{,}}\hskip 1.66669pt\mspace{3muplus0muminus3mu}}{\textup{,}\mspace{2muplus0muminus2mu}}{\textup{,}\mspace{1muplus0muminus1mu}}g\in G\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Let
\mathscr{S}
be the collection of all subsystems of
\langle Z,(G,\alpha)\rangle\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Define, for
X\in\mathscr{S}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\spec(A_{X})\coloneqq\overline{\bigcup_{x\in X}\spec(A_{x})}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Proposition 5.1

(Lemma
3.2
(
b
))
.

Assume
\langle G,\lambda\rangle
is unimodular.
Fix
x,y\in Z
and
\psi\in L^{2}(G)
and
j\in G\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Then

\displaystyle\SwapAboveDisplaySkip\lVert A_{x}\psi\rVert\leq

\displaystyle(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh)\lVert\psi\rVert\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\lVert A_{x}\!{}^{*}\psi\rVert\leq

\displaystyle(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh)\lVert\psi\rVert\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\lVert(A_{y}-A_{x})\psi\rVert\leq

\displaystyle(\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh)\lVert\psi\rVert\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle\lVert(A_{y}-A_{x})^{*}\psi\rVert\leq

\displaystyle(\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh)\lVert\psi\rVert\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\displaystyle U_{j^{\smash{-1}}}A_{x}U_{j}=

\displaystyle A_{j^{\smash{-1}}x}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Proof.
Observe

\displaystyle\lVert A_{x}\psi\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\int\lvert\int k(h,g^{\smash{-1}}x)\psi(gh)dh\rvert^{2}dg

\displaystyle\leq\int(\int\lvert k(h,g^{\smash{-1}}x)\rvert\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert dh)^{2}dg

\mathllap{\smash{(\begin{subarray}{l}\textrm{Hölder's}\\
\textrm{inequality}\end{subarray})}}

\displaystyle\leq\int(\int\lvert k(h,g^{\smash{-1}}x)\rvert\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(gh)dh)(\int\lvert k(h,g^{\smash{-1}}x)\rvert\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert^{2}dh)dg

\displaystyle\leq\int(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh)(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert^{2}dh)dg

\displaystyle=(\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh)^{2}\lVert\psi\rVert^{2}

and

\displaystyle\lVert A_{x}\!{}^{*}\psi\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\int\lvert\int\overline{k({h^{\smash{-1}}},{h^{\smash{-1}}g^{\smash{-1}}}x)}\psi(gh)dh\rvert^{2}dg

\displaystyle\leq\int(\int\lvert\overline{k({h^{\smash{-1}}},{h^{\smash{-1}}g^{\smash{-1}}}x)}\rvert\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert dh)^{2}dg

\displaystyle\leq\int(\int\lvert k({h^{\smash{-1}}},{(gh)^{\smash{-1}}}x)\rvert\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(gh)dh)(\int\lvert k({h^{\smash{-1}}},{(gh)^{\smash{-1}}}x)\rvert\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert^{2}dh)dg

\displaystyle\leq\int(\int\lVert k({h^{\smash{-1}}},{(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh)(\int\lVert k({h^{\smash{-1}}},{(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\lvert\psi(gh)\rvert^{2}dh)dg

\displaystyle=(\int\lVert k({h^{\smash{-1}}},{(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}}x)\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh)^{2}\lVert\psi\rVert^{2}

and

\displaystyle\lVert(A_{y}-A_{x})\psi\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\int\lvert\int(k(h,{g^{\smash{-1}}}y)-k(h,{g^{\smash{-1}}}x))\psi(gh)dh\rvert^{2}dg

\displaystyle\leq(\int\lVert(k(h,{(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}}y)-k(h,{(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)h^{\smash{-1}}}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh)^{2}\lVert\psi\rVert^{2}

and

\displaystyle\lVert(A_{y}-A_{x})^{*}\psi\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\int\lvert\int(\overline{k({h^{\smash{-1}}},{h^{\smash{-1}}g^{\smash{-1}}}y)}-\overline{k({h^{\smash{-1}}},{h^{\smash{-1}}g^{\smash{-1}}}x)})\psi(gh)dh\rvert^{2}dg

\displaystyle\leq(\int\lVert(k({h^{\smash{-1}}},(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)-k({h^{\smash{-1}}},(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{\supp(\psi)}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh)^{2}\lVert\psi\rVert^{2}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Also,

\textstyle U_{j^{\smash{-1}}}A_{x}U_{j}=\int d\lambda(h)U_{j^{\smash{-1}}}[k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x)]U_{j}R_{h}=\int d\lambda(h)[k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}j^{\smash{-1}}x)]R_{h}=A_{j^{\smash{-1}}x}\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}\qed

Proposition 5.2

(Lemma
3.2
(
c
))
.

Assume
\langle G,\lambda\rangle
is unimodular,
A_{x}
is normal for every
x\in Z\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
and the following.

(i)

k
satisfies a continuity condition:
for each
x\in Z
and for each
\varepsilon>0
and for each nonempty compact subset
F
of
G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

{\int\lvert k(h,y)-k(h,x)\rvert\mathbbm{1}_{F}(h)dh}<\varepsilon
for every
y
in some neighborhood of
x\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(ii)

k
satisfies a decay condition:
for each
x\in Z
and for each
\varepsilon>0\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
there exists a nonempty compact subset
F
of
G
such that

\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)\mathbbm{1}_{G\setminus F}(h)\rVert_{\infty}dh<\varepsilon
for every
y
in some neighborhood of
x\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Fix
x\in Z\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Then for each
\varepsilon>0\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\sup_{E\in\spec(A_{x})}\dist(E,\spec(A_{y}))<\varepsilon
for every
y
in some neighborhood of
x\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
i.e.,

\textstyle\lim_{y\xrightarrow{\smash{Z}}x}\sup_{E\in\spec({A}_{x})}\dist(E,\spec({A}_{y}))=0\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Proof.
It suffices to show that
for each
E\in\spec(A_{x})
and for each
\varepsilon>0\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

\dist(E,\spec(A_{y}))<\varepsilon
for every
y
in some neighborhood of
x

since
\spec(A_{x})
is compact.
Fix
E\in\spec(A_{x})
and
\varepsilon>0\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Without loss of generality, assume
E\notin\spec(A_{y})\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe there exists a nonempty compact subset
F
of
G
such that

\textstyle\int\lVert k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)\mathbbm{1}_{G\setminus F}(h)\rVert_{\infty}dh<\smash{\frac{1}{4}}\varepsilon

for every
y
in some neighborhood
U
of
x
since
k
satisfies a decay condition.
Observe
A_{x}
is normal and, by Weyl’s criterion, there exists a nonzero vector
\varphi
such that
\lVert(A_{x}-E)\varphi\rVert<\smash{\frac{1}{4}}\varepsilon\lVert\varphi\rVert\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Without loss of generality, assume
\varphi
is both continuous and compactly-supported.
Define
K\coloneqq\supp(\varphi)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

Observe

\textstyle\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{K}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\mathbbm{1}_{F}(h)dh<\smash{\frac{1}{4}}\varepsilon

for every
y
in some neighborhood
U^{\prime}
of
x

since the group action is continuous and
k
satisfies a continuity condition.
Observe for each
y\in U\cap U^{\prime}\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}

A_{y}
is normal and

\textstyle\lVert(A_{y}-E)^{-1}\rVert^{-1}\lVert\varphi\rVert\leq\lVert(A_{y}-E)\varphi\rVert=\lVert(A_{y}-E)^{*}\varphi\rVert<(\lVert(A_{y}-A_{x})^{*}\smash{\frac{\varphi}{\lVert\varphi\rVert}}\rVert+\smash{\frac{1}{4}}\varepsilon)\lVert\varphi\rVert

and

\displaystyle\dist(E,\spec(A_{y}))

\displaystyle=\lVert(A_{y}-E)^{-1}\rVert^{-1}

\displaystyle<\lVert(A_{y}-A_{x})^{*}\smash{\frac{\varphi}{\lVert\varphi\rVert}}\rVert+\smash{\frac{1}{4}}\varepsilon

\mathllap{\smash{(\begin{subarray}{l}\textrm{Prop~\ref{prop:A1(1)}}\end{subarray})}}

\displaystyle\leq\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{K}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}dh+\smash{\frac{1}{4}}\varepsilon

\displaystyle<\int\lVert(k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}y)-k(h,(\cdot)^{\smash{-1}}x))\mathbbm{1}_{K}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\mathbbm{1}_{F}(h)dh+\smash{\frac{3}{4}}\varepsilon

\displaystyle<\varepsilon\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}\qed

Remark 5.2.1
.

(a)

To see how conditions
(
i
)
and
(
ii
)
follow from the setting for Lemma
3.2
,
recall the setting has
f_{(\cdot)}\in C_{c}(G,C_{b}(Z))
and
f_{h}(x)=k(h,x)\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}

(b)

To see how
\spec(A_{x})=\spec(A_{X(x)})
follows from Proposition
5.1
and Proposition
5.2
,
if
y=j^{\smash{-1}}x
for some
j\in G\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
then
\spec(A_{y})=\spec(A_{x})
since

A_{y}=U_{j^{\smash{-1}}}A_{x}U_{j}
and
if
y
is a limit-point of the orbit of
x\mathchoice{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{,\kern-3.79996pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule[-1.94247pt]{1.47774pt}{1.1611pt}}\hss}\kern 2.13324pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss,}}{\text{,}}{\text{,}}
then
\spec(A_{y})\subseteq\spec(A_{x})
since

\inf_{j\in G}\sup_{E\in\spec(A_{y})}\dist(E,\spec(A_{j^{\smash{-1}}x}))=0\mathchoice{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{.\kern-2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\smash{\rule{1.26663pt}{1.26663pt}}\hss}\kern 2.11108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss.}}{\text{.}}{\text{.}}
